Format was a strong independent predictor of accuracy: models exceeding 94 percent on multiple choice fell to 23.9 percent on open completion (Claude Opus 4.6) and 6.2 percent on error identification (Claude Sonnet 4.6). The motivation condition changed accuracy in a predominantly negative, family-dependent direction. These dissociations indicate that art-historical knowledge and the ability to deploy it are distinct capabilities, and that single-format benchmarks overestimate what models can reliably do. Mapping this capability profile is a precondition for responsible use of multimodal LLMs in art-historical scholarship, where tasks demand producing and manipulating content rather than selecting from fixed options.

</details>


### [39] [OpenSafeIntent: Evaluating Intent-Calibrated Safe Completion Across Dual-Use Prompt Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02047)
*Rheeya Uppaal,Seungwoo Lyu,Selina Sung,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OpenSafeIntent是一个通过控制意图变体（良性、双重用途、恶意）来评估模型在跨意图条件下行为校准能力的基准，揭示了传统基于独立提示的评估方式掩盖了模型在意图变化时的安全失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估依赖独立提示，无法有效衡量模型在面对相同任务但意图变化时的行为一致性，尤其是模型是否能在提供有用帮助的同时避免造成伤害。

Method: 构建OpenSafeIntent基准，为每个任务生成三种意图变体（良性、双重用途、恶意），控制任务本身不变，仅改变意图，从而评估模型在不同意图下的行为校准性。

Result: 模型在匹配的意图变体间经常失效；双重用途行为对改写敏感；高风险话题的顶层回答不可靠；将模糊请求重构为安全任务的响应更不易越界。

Conclusion: 安全补全行为应作为对受控任务变体的意图校准行为来评估，而非基于独立提示的单一安全-有用性权衡。

Abstract: Safe completion requires models to provide useful assistance without enabling harm, but this behavior is difficult to evaluate with isolated prompts. We introduce OpenSafeIntent, a benchmark of controlled prompt-sets that vary intent while holding the underlying task fixed. Each datapoint contains benign, dual-use, and malicious variants of the same task. This design lets us evaluate whether models calibrate assistance across intent shifts, rather than merely appearing safe on average. Across a broad model suite, we find that prompt-level safety hides important failures: models often fail to remain safe across matched intent variants, dual-use behavior is brittle under paraphrase, high-level answers on risky topics are not reliably safe, and responses that reframe ambiguous requests into safer tasks are substantially less likely to cross the safety boundary. Our results suggest that safe completion should be evaluated as intent-calibrated behavior over controlled task variants, not as a single safety-helpfulness tradeoff over independent prompts.

</details>


### [40] [SPLIT: Cross-Lingual Empathy and Cultural Grounding in English and Ukrainian LLM Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02049)
*Anna Chorna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPLIT基准——包含500个提示，用于评估LLM在英语和乌克兰语的情感和文化支持一致性。实验发现Gemini-2.5-Flash和LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct转至乌克兰语时性能下降，DeepSeek-V3相对稳定；人类与AI评估者在同理心和自然度上弱一致，在文化根基上分歧显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准侧重多语言性能，但极少关注低资源语言在危机情境下的同理心和文化根基。LLM越来越多用于情感支持和危机场景，其跨语言能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建包含500个提示的SPLIT基准，覆盖压力、恐慌、孤独、内部流离、紧张五个类别。在三类LLM上评估三个维度：同理心准确性、语言自然度、语境与文化根基。采用LLM-as-a-jury范式进行自动评估并与人工评估对比。

Result: Gemini-2.5-Flash和LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct在转至乌克兰语时性能下降，DeepSeek-V3相对稳定；人类与AI评估者在同理心和自然度上弱一致，但在文化根基上存在分歧；生成乌克兰语文本不等同于生成乌克兰语情感支持。

Conclusion: 需要开发更具文化适应性的基准设计，并强调以人为中心的评估方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly deployed in emotional-support contexts and crisis-related situations. Nevertheless, their cross-lingual abilities in these circumstances remain underexplored. Existing benchmarks emphasize multilingual performance but rarely examine crisis-related empathy and cultural grounding in low-to-mid-resource languages. We introduce SPLIT, a 500-prompt benchmark designed to evaluate LLM consistency in generating emotionally grounded responses across five categories: Stress, Panic, Loneliness, Internal Displacement, and Tension. We evaluate three technically diverse LLMs across three dimensions: Empathetic Accuracy, Linguistic Naturalness, and Contextual & Cultural Grounding. The framework aims to assess and compare the quality of LLM responses in both English and Ukrainian languages, as well as to explore the reliability of the LLM-as-a-jury paradigm. Our findings reveal that Gemini-2.5-Flash and LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct degrade when transitioning to Ukrainian, while DeepSeek-V3 remains comparatively stable within our benchmark. We additionally find that human and AI evaluators agree weakly on empathy and naturalness but diverge on cultural grounding. We further argue that producing Ukrainian text is not equivalent to producing Ukrainian emotional support. Our findings may assist in the future development of more culturally tailored benchmark designs, as well as encourage a stronger emphasis on human-centered evaluation.

</details>


### [41] [Unlocking Speech-Text Compositional Powers: Instruction-Following Speech Language Models without Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02214)
*Congrui Du,Yang Zhang,Kaizhi Qian,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpeechCombine是一种无需指令微调的语音语言模型训练方法，仅通过30k小时数据的单轮语音预训练，将文本LLM的权重与指令微调产生的权重差直接组合，即可将文本LLM的能力迁移到语音领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SLM训练方法复制文本LLM范式，需要大规模合成语音预训练和指令微调数据集，但语音序列远长于文本序列，导致难以扩展。因此，需要一种摆脱对海量语音数据依赖的SLM训练新方向。

Method: 从基础文本LLM出发，先对语音话语进行连续预训练得到语音适配模型，然后将该模型权重与（指令微调后的文本LLM权重减去基础文本LLM权重）的权重差直接相加组合。

Result: 这种简单的权重组合策略不仅保留了原始文本LLM的知识和能力，还将其有效迁移到了语音领域，无需任何指令微调步骤。

Conclusion: 提出了SLM训练的新方向，避免了大规模语音数据的依赖，为更高效的语音语言模型训练提供了可能。

Abstract: Instruction tuning for speech language models (SLMs) is substantially more challenging than for text-based large language models (LLMs), as it requires learning a new modality and a wide range of speech-specific instructions in addition to those supported by text LLMs. Existing SLM training approaches largely replicate the text LLM training paradigm by synthesizing large-scale speech pre-training and instruction-tuning datasets. However, this strategy is difficult to scale, since speech sequences are significantly longer than text sequences. In this paper, we propose SpeechCombine, an instruction-following speech language model trained without any instruction tuning, using only a single round of speech pre-training on 30k hours of data. Starting from a text LLM base model, we perform continuous pre-training on speech utterances to obtain a speech-adapted model, and then directly combine its weights with the weight difference between the instruction-tuned and base versions of the text LLM. Our results show that this simple combination strategy not only preserves the knowledge and capabilities of the original text LLM, but also effectively transfers them to the speech domain. These findings suggest a new direction for SLM training that avoids reliance on massive speech data.

</details>


### [42] [Challenges and Recommendations for LLMs-as-a-Judge in Multilingual Settings and Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02235)
*A. Seza Doğruöz,Xixian Liao,Verena Blaschke,Jakob Prange,Senyu Li,David Ifeoluwa Adelani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了ACL Anthology中涉及多语言和低资源场景下LLM-as-a-Judge评估的论文，发现仅有33篇相关论文，且存在评估结果不一致、过度信任LLM判断、依赖单一评估模型等问题，最后提出了改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge已成为自然语言生成任务的主流评估范式，但在多语言特别是低资源语言场景下，LLM能力有限且缺乏充分的人工验证，现有研究对此问题的关注不足。

Method: 对ACL Anthology中提及LLM-as-a-Judge的650篇论文进行系统筛选，聚焦其中涉及多语言和低资源场景的33篇论文，进行深度分析。

Result: 仅33篇论文（占5%）关注多语言/低资源场景；这些论文存在评估结果不一致、过度信任LLM判断、广泛依赖单一评估模型等问题。

Conclusion: 当前多语言和低资源场景下LLM-as-a-Judge的使用存在显著问题，需要更加审慎的评估实践，作者为此提出了具体建议。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has become the dominant evaluation paradigm for many natural language generation tasks, due to shortcomings of conventional metrics and high correlations with human judgment, albeit mostly in English. There are now attempts to extend LLM-as-a-Judge to multilingual settings including low-resource languages. However, LLMs have limited proficiency in low-resource languages, and there is often no adequate human validation in these settings. To highlight the scope of the problem and current practices, we explore the use of LLM-as-a-Judge evaluators in ACL Anthology papers focusing on multilingual settings and low-resource languages across a diverse set of tasks. Out of 650 papers mentioning LLM-as-a-judge, only 33 of them focus on low-resource or multilingual settings. Our in-depth analysis of these papers indicates inconsistent evaluation outcomes, a tendency to overtrust LLM judgments in multilingual settings, and the widespread reliance on a single judge model per study. To help the NLP community further, we conclude with recommendations about how to use LLM-as-a-Judge in multilingual and low-resource settings.

</details>


### [43] [BamiBERT: A New BERT-based Language Model for Vietnamese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02259)
*Dat Quoc Nguyen,Thinh Pham,Chi Tran,Linh The Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BamiBERT是专为越南语设计的全新BERT预训练语言模型，在129GB语料上训练20个epoch，支持2048 tokens上下文长度，无需外部分词，在8个越南语基准测试的15项指标中取得11项最优、3项次优，达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越南语事实标准编码器PhoBERT存在关键局限性（如需要外部词分割、上下文长度有限），亟需新的预训练语言模型来克服这些问题。

Method: 从头训练BamiBERT模型，使用129GB通用领域越南语文本语料训练20个epoch，支持最长2048 tokens的扩展上下文长度，并且直接在原始输入上操作，不需要外部词分割。

Result: 在8个越南语基准测试的15项指标中，BamiBERT取得11项最高分、3项次高分，在"base"规模越南语编码器中达到新的SOTA，并展现出强大的跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: BamiBERT有效克服了PhoBERT的关键局限性，在多个基准测试上全面超越现有模型，为越南语自然语言处理提供了更优的基础编码器方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce BamiBERT, a new BERT-based pre-trained language model for Vietnamese that addresses key limitations of PhoBERT -- the current de facto Vietnamese text encoder. Trained from scratch on a 129GB corpus of general-domain Vietnamese text for 20 epochs, BamiBERT supports an extended context length of up to 2048 tokens and operates directly on raw input, eliminating the need for external word segmentation. Across 8 Vietnamese benchmarks, it achieves the best score on 11 of 15 metrics and the second-best on 3 others, setting a new state of the art among "base"-sized Vietnamese encoders and demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. We release BamiBERT at: https://huggingface.co/Qualcomm-AI-Research/BamiBERT

</details>


### [44] [CheckRLM: Effective Knowledge-Thought Coherence Checking in Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02262)
*Dingling Xu,Ruobing Wang,Qingfei Zhao,Yukun Yan,Zhichun Wang,Daren Zha,Shi Yu,Zhenghao Liu,Shuo Wang,Xu Han,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CheckRLM是一个通过检索增强生成（RAG）对推理过程中的事实错误进行及时检查与纠正的框架，能有效提升推理语言模型在知识密集型任务中的可靠性，并以更低成本缓解长程推理中的错误累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型（RLMs）在复杂任务上通过扩展推理链取得显著进步，但推理链中常包含事实错误，尤其在知识密集型任务中尤为突出，现有方法缺乏对推理过程中事实错误的有效检测与纠正机制。

Method: CheckRLM框架从推理链中提取事实性主张，识别和定位推理过程中的细微知识不一致；一旦检测到错误，通过引入外部知识的精炼机制以最小成本进行精准修正，确保推理链与正确知识之间的连贯性。

Result: 大量实验表明，CheckRLM显著优于现有基线方法，在长程推理中有效缓解了错误累积问题，且推理成本更低。

Conclusion: CheckRLM通过RAG实现推理过程中事实错误的及时检查与精确修正，为提升RLMs在知识密集型任务中的可靠性提供了一种高效、低成本的解决方案。

Abstract: Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) have significantly improved performance on complex tasks by extending the reasoning chain. However, these chains are prone to containing factual errors, particularly in knowledge-intensive tasks. To address this issue, we propose CheckRLM, a framework that improves the reliability of the reasoning process through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) by timely checking and correcting factual errors. Specifically, CheckRLM extracts factual claims from the reasoning chain to identify and localize subtle knowledge inconsistencies during inference. Upon detection of errors, a refinement mechanism performs minimal-cost yet precise corrections by leveraging external knowledge, ensuring coherence between the reasoning chain and correct knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CheckRLM substantially outperforms existing baselines, exhibiting a strong capability to mitigate error accumulation in long-horizon reasoning with lower costs. The code and data are available at https://github.com/AI9Stars/CheckRLM.

</details>


### [45] [On the Role of Directionality in Structural Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02307)
*Zichao Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于CCG有向类型的符号后端（确定性CKY+单线性解码器，仅3万可学习参数），在相同BERT-base编码器下，LF精确匹配率达75.9±6.4%，超越AM-Parser（70.8±4.3%）。方向性增益集中体现在位置偏移类别（+29.9pp），而递归深度类别仍依赖更强的编码器（DeBERTa-v3-large达90.7±4.9%）。方向性表示将瓶颈从符号层转移至神经层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA（AM-Parser）使用的AM代数操作不编码方向，而SLOG测试类别明确涉及方向性区分（如修饰语位置偏移、论元抽取位置），因此需要设计能显式编码方向的符号系统。

Method: 采用CCG有向类型作为符号后端，结合确定性CKY解析和单线性解码器，仅含3万可学习参数。在相同BERT-base编码器条件下与AM-Parser进行对比。

Result: CCG系统在LF精确匹配上达75.9±6.4%，超越AM-Parser的70.8±4.3%。在所有5个位置偏移类别上均优于AM-Parser（平均提升29.9pp），而AM-Parser在6个递归深度类别上更优。换用DeBERTa-v3-large编码器后达到90.7±4.9%，编码器提升主要作用于递归深度类别。

Conclusion: 方向性表示将性能瓶颈从符号层（AM-Parser的0%类别天花板）转移至神经层，后者可通过更强的编码器获得持续改善。

Abstract: Several SLOG test categories explicitly involve directional distinctions (modifier position shifts, argument extraction positions), yet AM-Parser, the previous SOTA, uses an AM algebra whose operations do not encode direction. We redesign the symbolic backend around CCG directed types (deterministic CKY + single linear decoder, 30K learnable parameters). Under the same BERT-base encoder, the system achieves 75.9$\pm$6.4% LF exact match, surpassing AM-Parser (70.8$\pm$4.3%). Per SLOG's own category groupings, gains are highly directional: the CCG system outperforms AM-Parser on all 5 position-shift categories (+29.9pp), while AM-Parser outperforms on all 6 recursive-depth categories. Replacing the encoder with DeBERTa-v3-large yields 90.7$\pm$4.9%, with the largest encoder gains in recursive-depth categories, complementary to directionality's gains. Directional representations shift the bottleneck from the symbolic layer (AM-Parser's 0% category ceiling) to the neural layer, which improves with encoder upgrades.

</details>


### [46] [World Wide Models: Literary Tools for Cultural AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02369)
*Nina Begus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出文学学科的工具（比较阅读、叙事学、诗学分析、批判理论、世界文学和翻译）对于构建具有文化素养的AI不可或缺，并发展了一个分层框架以促进更 nuanced 的文本模型和多元化的AI解读，通过宏观结构、流通和不可译性来应对全球AI文本性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM带来了大规模、自动化且单语的文化遭遇新形式，而现有的AI缺乏文化素养，文学学科的工具可以弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 发展了一个分层框架，强调文学与AI的自然交叉，将批判理论中的当前辩论与结构性单语主义联系起来，并应用世界文学的方法（宏观结构、流通、不可译性）来处理全球AI文本性。

Result: 提出了一个连接文学理论与AI发展的整合性框架，为解决AI的结构性单语主义问题提供了世界文学视角的解决方案。

Conclusion: 文学学科的工具对于构建文化素养高的AI至关重要，通过世界文学方法（宏观结构、流通、不可译性）可以有效应对全球AI文本性的挑战。

Abstract: LLMs stage a new form of cultural encounter that is massive, automated, and monolingual. Literary disciplines have always negotiated cultural struggles with comparative reading of literature, narratological and poetic analysis, critical theory, world literature, and translation. These tools have now become indispensable for building culturally literate AI. The essay develops a layered framework toward more nuanced textual models and pluralistic interpretations of AI, emphasizing the natural intersections of literature and AI development, connecting current debates in critical theory with structural monolingualism, and suggesting a new application of world literature approaches to address global AI textuality through macrostructure, circulation, and untranslatability.

</details>


### [47] [HULAT2 at MER-TRANS 2026: Governed Multi-Agent Simplification for Spanish Easy-to-Read Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02381)
*Lourdes Moreno,Paloma Martínez,Marco Antonio Sanchez-Escudero,Miguel Domínguez-Gómez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HULAT2-UC3M团队在MER-TRANS 2026西班牙语易读翻译任务中的参与情况。团队提交了三个全自动西班牙语系统：RUN1和RUN2采用基于LangGraph的多智能体工作流（使用Gemini 2.5 Flash和RigoChat-7B-v2），RUN1为基础版本，RUN2增加了词汇支持层；RUN3为基于RigoChat的生成-评估-再生成基线系统。官方SARI得分显示，RUN1（44.0543）最优，RUN2（43.1049）次之，RUN3（38.5136）最差。结果表明信号引导的多智能体路由优于线性再生成基线，且词汇支持层未能自动提升参考指标得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与MER-TRANS 2026共享任务，探索在西班牙语易读翻译中利用多智能体工作流和信号引导机制提升翻译质量的方法。

Method: 提出三种方案：RUN1和RUN2基于LangGraph的多智能体工作流，结合Gemini 2.5 Flash和RigoChat-7B-v2，采用并行生成策略、内部质量信号、事件-条件-动作路由、受控编辑和可追溯决策；RUN2额外增加基于词汇表和词汇资源的词汇支持层。RUN3为RigoChat基线的生成-评估-再生成流程，结合提示工程和LoRA适配。

Result: 官方SARI得分：RUN1为44.0543分，RUN2为43.1049分，RUN3为38.5136分。RUN1为最优系统。

Conclusion: 信号引导的多智能体路由优于线性再生成基线；词汇支持层的加入未能自动提升参考指标得分；需要进一步在片段级和文档级分析可读性、事实一致性和用户导向的充分性。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of HULAT2-UC3M in the Spanish track of MER-TRANS 2026, a shared task on multilingual Easy-to-Read translation. Three fully automatic Spanish runs were submitted. RUN1 and RUN2 used a LangGraph-based multi-agent workflow combining Gemini 2.5 Flash and RigoChat-7B-v2, parallel generation strategies, internal quality signals, Event-Condition-Action routing, controlled editing and traceable decisions. RUN1 used the base workflow, while RUN2 activated an additional lexical-support layer based on a glossary and lexical resources. RUN3 was a RigoChat-based generate-evaluate-regenerate baseline with prompt engineering and LoRA-based adaptation. The official leaderboard reports BLEU-Orig, BLEU-Gold, SARI and BERTScore. During development, additional internal signals were also inspected, including semantic fidelity, readability, lexical simplicity, syntactic clarity and factual consistency. According to official SARI, RUN1 was the best HULAT2 run, with 44.0543 points, followed by RUN2 with 43.1049 and RUN3 with 38.5136. These results indicate that, in this task setting, signal-guided multi-agent routing outperformed the linear regeneration baseline. They also show that adding lexical support did not automatically improve reference-based scores. Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.

</details>


### [48] [Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02383)
*Benjamin Nichols,Michael Schlichtkrull,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MEDIAREF是一个公开可用的网络文档知识库，支持可重复、低成本的媒体背景核查（MBC）生成，用于提升检索增强型事实核查系统的源批判性推理能力，解决了现有方法依赖昂贵专有搜索API、缺乏可重复性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的自动事实核查系统往往假设检索到的证据是可靠的，但现实中的信息可能存在冲突、过时或来自不可靠来源。虽然源批判性推理通过媒体背景核查（MBC）来评估证据来源的可信度，但生成MBC依赖昂贵的专有搜索API，限制了方法的可重复性。为此，需要构建一个公开可用、低成本的知识库来支持MBC的生成与评估。

Method: 构建MEDIAREF——一个覆盖200个媒体来源的网络文档公开知识库；提出可重复的方法论用于构建和更新该语料库；评估多种主流LLM在MBC生成任务上的表现。

Result: MEDIAREF能够支持更高质量的MBC生成，并通过自动评估和定性评估得到验证。

Conclusion: MEDIAREF通过提供可重复、低成本的知识库，有效解决了现有MBC生成依赖专有API的问题，为源批判性推理在自动事实核查中的应用提供了更可靠的支撑。

Abstract: LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.

</details>


### [49] [The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02416)
*David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NLP研究的发表重心正从ACL等核心学科会议向通用ML会议迁移，这一趋势在作者群体和引用影响上均有体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM兴起后，NLP研究的学科发表重心是否从传统NLP会议向通用ML会议迁移。

Method: 分析2010-2026年NLP论文数据，对比LLM前后时期，分别研究资深作者和新作者（以至少3篇NLP一作论文为门槛）的发表分布变化，并使用因果推断技术评估通用ML会议对引用的影响。

Result: 1）资深作者在*ACL主会份额下降19.2pp，在Findings上升14.8pp，在通用ML会议上升8.6pp；2）新作者中主要发表*ACL类会议的比例从84%降至74%，主要发表通用ML会议的比例从5%升至21%；3）通用ML会议存在显著的引用溢价。

Conclusion: NLP研究发表重心正显著向通用ML会议迁移，引用优势是驱动此迁移的因素之一。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.

</details>


### [50] [Language Models as Measurement Apparatus for Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02459)
*Kent K. Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证NLP对文化的量化测量是一种物质-话语实践：测量装置（模型、数据、标注、评估）并非被动记录文化现实，而是参与建构了它所测量的文化现实。借鉴Karen Barad的"能动切割"概念，通过电视电影对话的三个案例研究和三个装置审视，提出一种理论驱动、经验严谨、文化偶然的研究纲领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被用于量化文化现象，但学界对"什么使得这种测量具有文化独特性"缺乏深入反思。现有工作默认文化测量是一种客观记录，忽略了测量装置本身参与建构文化现实这一本体论问题。

Method: 采用理论分析与案例研究结合的方法。理论层面引入Karen Barad的"能动切割"（agential cut）概念；实证层面通过三个电视电影对话案例（测量结构、交互、偏差）和三个装置审视（文化标记的抹除、对历史材料的调谐、能动工作流中的主体性）进行论证。

Result: 揭示了文化测量中装置与现象之间的边界不是预先给定的，而是由设计选择能动地划定的；语言模型已内化了大量它所测量的文化材料，因此这种边界从一开始就是纠缠的。六个具体案例分析展示了这种纠缠在不同维度的表现。

Conclusion: 提出未来的文化NLP研究应当是有理论驱动、经验严谨且文化偶然的研究纲领，将每一次"能动切割"视为同时具有方法论和伦理学意义的自觉承诺。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to quantify cultural phenomena, but what makes such measurement distinctively cultural? This paper argues that NLP work on culture is a material-discursive practice: the apparatus -- model, data, annotation, evaluation -- participates in constituting the cultural reality it measures, rather than passively recording it. Drawing on Karen Barad's concept of the agential cut -- the contingent boundary between phenomenon and instrument -- I show that the apparatus's substantive design choices draw such boundaries, and that the boundary is entangled from the start because language models have already internalized much of the cultural material they measure. I illustrate this through three case studies on television and film dialogue (measuring structure, interaction, and deviation) and three examinations of the apparatus itself (erasure of cultural markers, attunement to historical material, and agency in an agentic workflow). This big picture analysis proposes a research program that is theory-driven, empirically rigorous, and culturally contingent, treating each agential cut as a conscious commitment, at once methodological and ethical.

</details>


### [51] [Will Scaling Improve Social Simulation with LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02464)
*Caleb Ziems,William Held,Su Doga Karaca,David Grusky,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过缩放定律研究85个Qwen3架构的Transformer LLM（计算预算10^18-10^20 FLOPs）和35个开放权重模型（最高70B参数），发现大多数社交模拟任务（观点建模、行为模拟、纵向预测）的保真度随计算规模提升而显著改进，但纵向预测、欠代表性群体观点缩放较慢，且行为模拟中模型对人类认知偏差（如风险厌恶）的校准不随规模提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM社交模拟的保真度是否随模型规模提升而自然改善，还是与通用能力正交而需要专门研究。

Method: 使用缩放定律分析85个Qwen3 Transformer LLM（固定计算预算10^18-10^20 FLOPs）和35个开放权重模型（最高70B参数），在观点建模、行为模拟、纵向预测三个子领域评估社交模拟保真度，并预测下游准确率与损失的关系。

Result: 大多数社交模拟任务随规模提升快速改善，尤其对于英语语料库中充分代表的群体；纵向预测和欠代表性观点缩放较慢；行为模拟中对风险厌恶等认知偏差的校准不随规模提升（0.5B到8B参数均无改善）。

Conclusion: 规模提升在大多数场景下能改善社交模拟保真度，但存在异常值，低资源领域的改进不可靠。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) social simulations are a promising research method, but they are not yet faithful enough to be adopted widely. In this work, we investigate whether the current scaling paradigm in language modeling is likely to close these gaps, or whether simulation fidelity is orthogonal to general capabilities and therefore deserving of more research attention. We use scaling laws to study the relationship between LLMs' compute scale, general capability benchmarks, and the fidelity of social simulation in three representative sub-domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Surprisingly, we discover strong compute scaling in all three settings, using a suite of 85 transformer LLMs with the Qwen3 architecture pre-trained on the DCLM web text corpus under fixed-compute budgets from $10^{18}$ to $10^{20}$ FLOPs. Then we evaluate 35 larger and more capable open-weight models up to 70B parameters, allowing us to predict downstream accuracy from loss. This reveals that the majority of behavioral and opinion simulation tasks will rapidly improve with scale, particularly when they involve populations that are well-represented in English web corpora. Longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions scale more slowly, especially when they are less correlated with general knowledge and reasoning benchmarks like MMLU. In behavior simulation, scaling fails to improve model calibration with human cognitive biases like risk aversion, as well as human heuristics like learning correlated rewards from related tasks. On these tasks, even fine-tuned models fail to noticeably scale up performance from 0.5B to 8B parameters. Taken together, we conclude that scale will improve social simulations in most settings, but outliers exist, and improvements will be less reliable in low-resource domains.

</details>


### [52] [Audio-Based Understanding of Audiobook Narration Appeal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02473)
*Shahar Elisha,Mariano Beguerisse-Díaz,Emmanouil Benetos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过提取语音和声学特征（如语调、节奏、响度）研究有声书叙述质量如何影响听众参与度（播放率），发现声学信息与内容吸引力有稳健关联，且效果因体裁和书名而异。这是首个系统性计算研究，将叙述质量、体裁、书名与有声书消费联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙述质量对有声书听众体验至关重要，但其影响效果因体裁、书名和受众而异，目前缺乏大规模计算研究来量化这种关系。

Method: 从LibriVox中提取语音和声学特征（语调、节奏、响度等），使用预训练音频模型进行分析，并与消费数据（播放率）进行关联分析，同时控制体裁和书名效应。研究结果通过更精细的专有参与度指标进行验证。

Result: 即使消费数据有限，声学信息单独就与内容吸引力表现出稳健关联，且在控制书名效应后依然成立。不同体裁和书名下，声学特征的影响存在差异。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性地将叙述质量、体裁、书名与有声书消费联系起来的大规模计算研究，展示了数据驱动方法在改善有声书个性化和叙述者选角方面的潜力。

Abstract: Narration is central to the audiobook listening experience, shaping how listeners engage with and understand the content. This work explores how narration qualities shape an audiobook's appeal, noting that their effects can vary by genre, title, and audience. We extract vocal and acoustic features (e.g., tone, pace, loudness) from LibriVox using pre-trained audio models and analyse their relationship with consumption data (specifically, view-rate) and their interplay with genre and title. Despite limited consumption data, we find that acoustic information alone has a robust association with appeal, even after accounting for title effects. We further validate these findings using more nuanced proprietary engagement metrics. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic computational study linking narration qualities, genre, title, and audiobook consumption, highlighting the potential of data-driven insights to improve audiobook personalisation and narrator casting.

</details>


### [53] [Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02490)
*Liyan Tang,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VRRL框架，通过随机掩码轨迹前缀和缓冲回滚机制，增强大视觉语言模型在反思阶段对视觉输入的关注，从而提升其在分布外场景下的视觉接地自我反思能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型在链式思维推理中具备自我反思能力，但在反思时未能充分关注视觉输入，导致无法将反馈转化为基于视觉的修正，尤其在分布外图像上表现不佳。

Method: 提出VRRL强化学习训练框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) 随机掩码轨迹前缀，强调从错误中间预测中恢复而非早期犯错；(2) 从经验回放缓冲区引入缓冲回滚，使模型暴露于需要学习修正的多样化失败状态。

Result: 在表格、图表视觉接地任务和空间导航基准上，VRRL显著提升了分布外准确率，优于标准强化学习和面向反思的微调基线方法。

Conclusion: VRRL通过有效利用自我反思，使模型在分布偏移下仍能保持高准确率，而现成模型和传统微调模型则表现大幅下降。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [54] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li,Lingxi Xie,Xinyue Huo,Jihao Qiu,Jiacheng Shao,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对长视频剧集中的说话人识别难题，提出包含53.2万条标注对话的大规模基准DramaSR-532K，以及基于大型推理模型的多模态工具调用方法DramaSR-LRM，在短语音场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频剧集的全面理解面临严峻挑战，其中说话人识别（准确将每句台词归因到对应角色）是解读复杂剧情的关键，但目前缺乏大规模基准和鲁棒方法。

Method: (1) 构建DramaSR-532K基准，包含532K标注对话、900+角色，融合听觉、语言和视觉线索；(2) 提出DramaSR-LRM方法，基于大型推理模型，通过多模态工具调用自主聚合上下文证据实现高保真归因。

Result: DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线方法，尤其在声纹生物特征本身不可靠的短语音场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 多模态工具调用结合大型推理模型能有效提升长视频剧集中的说话人识别性能，数据与代码将开源。

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}

</details>


### [55] [LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02513)
*Matteo Boglioni,Thibault Rousset,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LACUNA提出了首个具有参数级定位标注的遗忘测试平台，通过在预定义参数中注入合成PII来评估遗忘方法是否真正擦除知识。实验发现现有SOTA方法虽在输出层面表现良好，但定位精度差且易受复现攻击；而精确定位后，简单梯度方法也能实现稳健擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘基准仅在输出层面评估模型效果，无法判断是否真正从参数中擦除知识还是仅模糊化处理，而重激活攻击的成功进一步证实了这一担忧。因此需要引入参数级定位的测试平台来弥合评估缺口。

Method: 通过掩码连续预训练将合成个体的PII注入到OLMo系列模型（1B和7B）的预定义参数中，构建了首个具有参数级ground-truth定位的遗忘测试平台LACUNA，用于直接评估遗忘方法是否针对实际存储知识的权重进行操作。

Result: 现有SOTA遗忘方法在输出层面表现良好，但参数级定位不精确且易受重激活攻击；而当定位准确时，简单的基于梯度的遗忘方法也能实现强擦除效果和对重激活攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精确的参数级定位对于实现稳健的知识擦除至关重要，LACUNA可作为行为评估的补充基准，推动基于定位的遗忘方法进一步发展。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>

### [56] [Embedding Inference Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01276)
*Cedric Fitiavana Raelijohn,Sébastien Gambs,Jean-Francois Rajotte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌入推理攻击（EIA），在黑盒场景下（仅观察检索文档的无序集合），通过精心设计的查询识别IR系统所使用的嵌入模型，并在包含重排序器或RAG系统的场景下验证了攻击有效性，同时探讨了相似度阈值等缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入反转攻击通常需要攻击者知晓嵌入模型，但实际IR系统通常隐藏于API之后。本文旨在研究更严格的**黑盒设定**下（仅能观察到检索文档的无序集合，无排序及相似度分数），攻击者能否识别出系统所使用的嵌入模型。

Method: 1. 从已知候选模型集合中，构造**区分性查询**，使得不同嵌入模型对同一查询的检索结果产生可区分的文档集合。2. 在包含**重排序器**的场景下验证查询的区分性是否保持。3. 在真实**RAG系统**中，利用LLM对特定形式查询的接受特性绕过其输入过滤。4. 提出并评估**相似度阈值**作为缓解策略。

Result: 1. 在黑盒设定下，攻击者能够通过精心设计的查询有效识别出目标嵌入模型。2. 即使系统加入重排序器，部分查询仍保持区分性。3. 在真实RAG系统中，攻击查询能够绕过LLM的输入拒绝机制。4. 相似度阈值等缓解策略可一定程度上降低攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 本文首次揭示了在黑盒检索场景下嵌入模型可以被识别，暴露了IR系统的新安全风险，并提供了相应的防御思路。

Abstract: Embedding models are essential components of modern Information Retrieval (IR) systems, yet they are typically hidden behind APIs. Recent works have shown that dense IR system can lead to security vulnerabilities such as embedding inversion attacks. However, such attacks usually require that the attacker knows the embedding model for the attack to be applicable. In this paper, we study IR systems under a black-box setting in which the adversary observes only the unordered set of retrieved documents, without ranking or similarity scores. We demonstrate that in such contexts, tailored queries allow an adversary to identify which embedding model is in use from a set of known model candidate, which we coin as an embedding inference attack (EIA). We also show that certain queries remain discriminative even when the system includes a reranker as a potential defense mechanism. We further validate our method on a real Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system, in which the tailored queries bypass the LLM's tendency to reject inputs it does not recognize as well-formed questions. Finally, we propose and evaluate other mitigation strategies such as similarity thresholds.

</details>


### [57] [Cognitive Firewall: A Proactive, Zero-Trust, Multi-Gate Framework for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01277)
*Michele Guida,Ruslan Shikhhamzayev,Sindhuja Penchala,Stefano Iannucci,Jiacheng Li,Shahram Rahimi,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cognitive Firewall，一种用于大语言模型的主动运行时监督框架，通过四个分类门（意图门、零信任上下文门、一致性门、输出风险门）对对话进行分解式安全评估，并采用升级机制而非分数平均来组合门决策。实验表明，该方法在多个越狱基准测试上将攻击成功率降至2%以下，最难的人工构建攻击集上降至14%，同时过拒率仅8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时安全机制通常将提示或响应作为孤立消息评估，无法恢复跨轮累积的恶意意图、验证声称的权限，或检测跨对话分解的有害目标，因此需要一种能够感知对话上下文的主动监督框架。

Method: Cognitive Firewall框架在用户与受保护目标模型之间插入一个独立的监督模型，将安全评估分解为四个分类门：1) 意图门——识别请求的操作目标；2) 零信任上下文门——将声称的角色和权限视为未经验证的证据；3) 一致性门——检测跨轮的升级与分解；4) 输出风险门——在发布前检查候选响应。门决策通过升级机制（而非分数平均）组合，任何置信的危险信号即可阻止交互并保留可审计理由。

Result: 在四个越狱基准测试和一个良性安全测试集上，Cognitive Firewall大幅降低了单轮、多轮、基于权威和人工构建的攻击成功率。三个攻击集上攻击成功率降至2%或以下，最难的人工构建攻击集上降至14%，过拒率维持在8%。

Conclusion: 分解式、对话级别的监督能够改进LLM安全的主动遏制能力和可审计性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be induced to produce harmful content through multi turn strategies in which no single user message appears clearly unsafe. Existing runtime safeguards commonly evaluate prompts or responses as isolated messages, which limits their ability to recover ac-cumulated intent, verify asserted authority, or detect harmful objectives decomposed across a dialogue. This paper presents the Cognitive Firewall, a proactive runtime oversight framework that interposes an independent oversight model between a user and a protected target mod l. The framework decomposes safety assessment into four categorical gates: an intent gate that identi-fies the operational objective of a request, a zero trust context gate that treats claimed roles and permissions as unverified evidence, a consistency gate that detects escalation and decomposition across turns, and an output risk gate that inspects candidate responses before release. Gate decisions are combined through escalation rather than score averaging, allowing any confident danger signal to block an interaction while preserving an auditable rationale. Experiments on four jailbreak benchmarks and a benign safety test set show that the Cognitive Firewall substantially reduces attack success across single turn, multi turn, authority based, and human crafted attacks. It lowers attack success to 2 percent or below on three attack sets and to 14 percent on the most difficult human crafted set, while maintaining an 8 percent over refusal rate. These results indicate that decomposed, conversation level oversight can improve proactive containment and auditability for LLM safety.

</details>


### [58] [Generative AI and Federated Learning for Intrusion Detection Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01305)
*Jiefei Liu,Abu Saleh Md Tayeen,Pratyay Kumar,Qixu Gong,Wenbin Jiang,Huiping Cao,Satyajayant Misra,Jayashree Harikumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了生成式AI与联邦学习在入侵检测系统中的应用，涵盖了生成模型（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM）的各类任务，以及FL在隐私保护下的分布式IDS训练，并探讨了合成数据质量、非IID分布、通信效率等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IDS面临攻击行为演化、数据集获取困难、数据不完整、类别不平衡及隐私约束等挑战，生成式AI和FL提供了缓解这些局限的新机遇。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，先总结IDS研究方向（对抗机器学习、异常检测、IoT-IDS、可解释IDS、基准数据集），再按模型族和任务目标分类生成式AI应用（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM），最后综述生成式AI与FL结合的IDS研究。

Result: 归纳了生成式AI在IDS中的多种应用方向：异常检测、合成流量生成、数据增强、数据补全、对抗流量生成及IDS告警解释；FL使得分布式IDS训练在不直接共享本地流量的情况下成为可能。

Conclusion: 指出了开放挑战：合成数据质量、真实流量生成、双用途对抗风险、非IID客户端分布、通信高效的模型共享、联邦IDS基准测试以及面向网络安全的领域专用LLM。

Abstract: Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs) are essential for monitoring network traffic and identifying malicious activities in modern cyber-physical, Internet of Things (IoT), enterprise, and distributed network environments. However, developing reliable IDS models remains challenging because attack behaviors evolve over time, realistic datasets are difficult to obtain, traffic records may be incomplete, attack classes are often imbalanced, and privacy constraints limit centralized data collection. Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and Federated Learning (FL) provide new opportunities to address these limitations. Generative models can support anomaly detection, synthetic traffic generation, data augmentation, data imputation, adversarial traffic generation, and IDS alert explanation. FL enables distributed IDS training without directly sharing local network traffic, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive and geographically distributed environments. This survey provides a structured review of generative AI and FL techniques for IDS. We first summarize representative IDS research directions, including adversarial machine learning, anomaly-based detection, IoT-oriented IDS, explainable IDS, and benchmark datasets. We then categorize generative AI applications in IDS according to model families and task objectives, covering autoencoder-based models, Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), diffusion models, and Large Language Models (LLMs). Finally, we review emerging studies that integrate generative AI with FL-based IDS and discuss open challenges, including synthetic data quality, realistic traffic generation, dual-use adversarial risks, non-IID client distributions, communication-efficient model sharing, federated IDS benchmarking, and domain-specific LLMs for network security.

</details>


### [59] [An alternative approach towards attacks against fully-split PLWE instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01340)
*Iván Blanco-Chacón,Rodrigo Martín Sánchez-Ledesma,Raúl Durán Díaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于根的量子攻击的泛化问题，证明了在完全分裂多项式环下，构造显式同构无法产生新的漏洞，因为这类同构会不可逆地扭曲样本，导致无法用于区分，并且证明了完全分裂多项式环之间的任何同构都必须具有所构造的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答作者先前工作中基于根的攻击的泛化问题，探讨通过构造脆弱实例的显式同构来扩展攻击的可能性。

Method: 首先构造完全分裂多项式环与可攻击多项式环之间的显式同构，证明该同构会扭曲样本使其无法用于区分；然后证明完全分裂多项式环之间的任何同构都必须具有该构造形式。

Result: 证明了在完全分裂设定下，通过构造显式同构的方法不会产生新的漏洞。

Conclusion: 基于根的攻击在完全分裂多项式环设定下无法通过同构方法进一步泛化，该攻击途径在该设定下是封闭的。

Abstract: In the present work we address some key questions regarding the generalization of root-based attacks presented in a recent work by the authors. In particular, we analyze potential root-based attacks extensions via the construction of explicit isomorphisms from vulnerable instances, and provide a formal proof that this approach will not yield any new vulnerabilities under a fully-split setting. To do so, we first construct an explicit isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings and polynomial rings where previous attacks apply and show that the application of such an isomorphism will always distort the samples in a way that the resulting samples cannot be used to distinguish. Then, we prove that any isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings must be of the form of the constructed isomorphism.

</details>


### [60] [Chameleon: Recovering Cyber-Physical Systems from Memory Corruption Attacks via ML Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01356)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Chameleon框架，利用机器学习生成的代理模块在检测到内存破坏攻击时替换受损组件，实现CPS系统的自动恢复而非简单终止，在7种机器人车辆上验证有效（平均R²=0.96）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS安全技术检测到攻击后只能终止或重启系统，这对安全关键型任务可能造成灾难性后果；而替换为简化默认值会降级系统行为。需要一种能在攻击发生后仍保证系统持续运行的恢复机制。

Method: 基于LLVM编译器实现，以分区（compartment）为粒度训练机器学习代理模型，使其精确模仿原始分区的行为但不继承内存破坏漏洞；攻击检测后自动将受损分区替换为训练好的代理。

Result: 在7种不同机器人车辆（含仿真和真实）上评估，代理模型与原始分区的拟合度平均R²=0.96；能成功从真实世界内存破坏攻击中恢复系统并完成任务；性能与内存开销低。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过ML代理替代受损分区的方式，实现了CPS在遭受内存破坏攻击后的有效恢复，避免了传统终止/重启策略的弊端，为安全关键型CPS提供了可行的攻击恢复方案。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPSs) are increasingly deployed in every aspect of our lives and can be compromised through memory corruption vulnerabilities, allowing attackers to hijack the control flow and take over the system. Existing techniques mostly focus on detecting such attacks but respond by terminating or halting execution upon attack detection, which is not acceptable in CPSs used in safety-critical tasks, as interrupted tasks can have catastrophic consequences. Other techniques replace compromised CPS components with simplified defaults that degrade system behavior, or reboot the system upon attack detection.
  We propose Chameleon, a novel framework for automatically recovering CPSs from memory corruption attacks using machine learning (ML)-based surrogates trained at compartment granularity that nearly replicate their original compartments' behavior but do not have the same memory corruption vulnerabilities. Upon attack detection, Chameleon replaces the compromised compartment with its trained surrogate. We implemented Chameleon using the LLVM compiler and evaluated its efficiency and effectiveness on seven different robotic vehicles (RVs), including simulated and real ones. We found that Chameleon can generate surrogates that closely approximate the original compartments (with an average R$^2$=0.96), successfully recover the system despite real-world memory corruption attacks unlike prior approaches, and complete their tasks while incurring low performance and memory overhead.

</details>


### [61] [From Forgeries to Foundation Models: A Systematic Survey of Identity Document Attack and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01442)
*Gourab Das,Pavan Kumar C,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次统一了针对身份伪造的三种攻击形式（物理呈现攻击、数字注入攻击和生成式AI合成攻击）的威胁模型，系统回顾了从规则启发式到少样本框架的检测方法，并通过数据集审计和零样本基准测试揭示了现有方法在跨域泛化上的严重不足——即使最强模型在安全操作条件下APCER仍高于25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具大幅降低了身份文档伪造的技术门槛，使高保真合成和字段级篡改变得容易，而现有检测方法所依赖的基准测试无法反映这一真实威胁，导致评估与实战之间存在严重的现实鸿沟（Reality Gap）。

Method: 采用统一的威胁模型覆盖三种攻击形式；系统梳理从规则启发式到少样本框架的检测方法论演进；对2019–2025年公开数据集进行系统性审计；分析大多模态模型在身份文档操纵中的表现，提出脚本依赖生成不稳定性（SDGI）概念；在未见过的合成ID卡上进行零样本基准测试。

Result: 检测方法在基准条件下表现良好，但零样本测试显示最强模型在安全操作条件下APCER仍高于25%；数据集审计暴露了基准与实战之间的持续差距；发现非拉丁字母修复中存在SDGI这一系统性失败模式。

Conclusion: 亟需构建具有法医基础、隐私保护和法律问责能力的身份验证系统，未来方向应聚焦于跨域泛化能力的提升和真实场景适应性的增强。

Abstract: Identity document forgery has undergone a fundamental capability shift: generative AI tools now enable high-fidelity document synthesis and field-level manipulation with minimal technical expertise, while detection methods remain constrained by benchmarks that do not reflect this threat. The resulting attack surface spans physical presentation, digital injection, and fully generative synthesis, introducing distinct forensic failure modes that require a unified threat model and evaluation framework. This survey provides, to our knowledge, the first unified treatment of Presentation Attacks, Digital Injection Attacks, and GenAI-driven synthesis within a single identity verification threat model. We trace detection methodologies from rule-based heuristics through forensic localisation, injection-aware pipelines, foundation models, and few-shot frameworks. A systematic audit of public datasets from 2019--2025 exposes a persistent Reality Gap between benchmark conditions and operational deployment. We further analyse large multimodal models for identity document manipulation, identifying Script-Dependent Generative Instability (SDGI) as a recurring typographic failure mode in non-Latin script inpainting. Finally, zero-shot benchmarking on unseen synthesised ID cards shows that even the strongest publicly available models achieve APCER values above 25% under security-oriented operating conditions, highlighting substantial limits in cross-domain generalisation. We conclude by outlining future directions toward forensically grounded, privacy-preserving, and legally accountable identity verification systems.

</details>


### [62] [Hamm-Grams: An Algorithm for Mining Regular Expressions of Bytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01445)
*Derek Everett,Edward Raff,James Holt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为hamm-grams的新型鲁棒特征（固定长度的正则表达式，含单字符通配符），用于恶意软件检测与分类，并设计了基于局部敏感哈希的高效算法来提取这些特征，实验证明了其相对于传统n-gram特征的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的n-gram特征在恶意软件检测中虽然常用，但其本质上脆弱（brittle），容易因恶意软件的微小变异而失效。因此需要构造更鲁棒的特征来应对恶意软件不断演化的威胁。

Method: 提出hamm-grams特征——一类固定长度且带有单字符通配符的正则表达式。设计了一种新的局部敏感哈希（LSH）算法，使得汉明距离较小的特征对容易产生哈希碰撞，并在哈希桶内进行聚类以确定通配符位置，从而高效地挖掘常见的hamm-gram特征。

Result: 在恶意软件分类和检测任务上验证了hamm-grams特征相比传统n-gram特征具有更好的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: hamm-grams作为一种更具鲁棒性的静态特征，能够有效提升恶意软件检测系统的性能，为应对恶意软件演化提供了新的特征构造思路。

Abstract: Malware poses a critical and ever-evolving threat, and robust and effective systems for detecting and classifying malware are of essential importance. $n$-grams features are among the common static features used in effective machine learning systems for malware, but these features are inherently brittle. We propose an algorithm for constructing more robust features, hamm-grams, which are a special class of regular expressions having a fixed length and single-character wildcards. We devise an efficient algorithm for finding common hamm-grams using a new locality-sensitive hash designed to produce collisions among pairs of small Hamming distance and a clustering within hash buckets to place wildcards. We then demonstrate the advantages of these features in malware classification and detection tasks.

</details>


### [63] [Overthink-Triggered Slowdown Attacks on LVLM-Based Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01518)
*Qiang Han,Jie Wu,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并验证了大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）在机器人系统中存在"过度思考"行为，即生成过长的推理链导致推理时间激增。攻击者可在视觉场景中嵌入精心设计的可读文本，触发过度思考，从而对机器人系统实施"过度思考诱导的慢速攻击"。作者提出了三阶段框架来系统识别和验证可迁移的触发词，实验表明在三个LVLM上所有触发词均产生>1.0x的减速比，最强单触发词达6.96x，物理打印文本仍能造成4.74x的延迟放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在机器人系统中存在过度思考行为，产生过长的推理轨迹导致推理时间激增。这种过度思考行为存在安全风险：攻击者可以故意触发过度思考来减缓受害机器人系统的决策速度，引发多种安全问题（即过度思考诱导的慢速攻击）。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1) 构建推理密集型场景文本语料库，从短响应前缀中提取与过度思考相关的词汇特征；2) 基于前缀代理分数进行高效黑盒搜索，并通过完整延迟测量确认少量顶级候选；3) 在未见图像和多个LVLM上使用固定触发词池评估黑盒迁移性，报告延迟放大倍数和攻击成功率。

Result: 在三个代表性LVLM上，所有触发词均产生大于1.0x的减速比，最强单触发词达6.96x。物理打印文本触发词仍能造成高达4.74x的延迟放大。触发词可在多个LVLM模型间迁移，持续导致机器人系统显著减速。

Conclusion: 本文发现的触发词可在多个LVLM模型间迁移，并一致地导致机器人系统显著减速，证实了该攻击的有效性和威胁性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have been increasingly integrated into robotic systems. However, these models may exhibit overthinking behaviors, where they generate excessively long reasoning traces, incurring an excessive inference time. This overthinking behavior poses a serious risk to robotic systems, as the adversary can deliberately trigger overthinking to slow down the decision making of a victim robotic system, causing a variety of safety issues (i.e., an overthinking-induced slowdown attack). To initiate this attack, an adversary can embed carefully crafted, human-readable scene text into the visual scene observed by a victim robotic agent, causing significant inference delays even under a strict black-box setting. Therefore, the embedded scene text serves as a significant "trigger" for the attack.
  This work systematically identifies and validates transferable triggers of overthinking in robotic systems by introducing a three-stage framework. First, we construct a diverse corpus of reasoning-intensive scene text and extract overthinking-correlated lexical features from short response prefixes. Second, we perform an efficient black-box search guided by a prefix-based proxy score while selectively confirming a small set of top candidates with full latency measurements. Third, we evaluate black-box transfer using a fixed pool of triggers on unseen images and multiple LVLMs, reporting latency amplification and attack success rates under standard thresholds. Across three representative LVLMs, all triggers yield slowdown ratios greater than 1.0x, with the strongest single-trigger case reaching 6.96x. The physical printing of the text trigger still causes up to 4.74x latency amplification. These results demonstrate that our discovered triggers are transferred between multiple LVLM models and consistently cause significant slowdowns in robotic systems.

</details>


### [64] [LIB-TRAP: Standard Cell Library Hardware Trojan Risk Assessment and Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01526)
*Harish Kumar Dharavath,Md Muhtasim Alam Chowdhury,Rozhin Yasaei,Soheil Salehi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型硬件木马威胁模型，将标准单元视为不可信来源。通过将标准单元库改造为恶意库，在集成电路设计中植入隐藏的硬件木马，并在制造阶段激活。在AES-128加密核、以太网控制器和WISHBONE DMA引擎等基准电路上验证了该攻击向量的有效性和隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注标准单元间空隙中的硬件木马插入，但缺乏对构成现代数字设计基础的标准单元本身脆弱性的研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索标准单元级别的HT威胁。

Method: 将现有标准单元库（Saed32nm和Sky130nm）改造为恶意库，其中包含失活的HT单元和对应的激活单元。设计端使用含失活HT单元的库进行综合与实现，制造阶段代工厂将失活单元替换为激活单元。使用开源和工业标准EDA工具，在Synopsys 32nm和SkyWater 130nm工艺上对多个基准电路进行综合。

Result: 在AES-128加密核、以太网控制器和WISHBONE DMA引擎三个基准电路中成功实现了标准单元级硬件木马攻击，提取了总单元数、总面积、动态功耗和静态功耗等设计级特征用于二元分类分析。

Conclusion: 标准单元本身可作为硬件木马攻击的载体，且具有高度隐蔽性，现有设计流程难以察觉。这一新型威胁模型揭示了fabless半导体制造模式中更深层次的安全漏洞。

Abstract: Vulnerabilities inherent to the fabless semiconductor manufacturing model have significantly increased the risk of malicious Hardware Trojan (HT) insertion, posing severe threats to hardware security. Several HT mitigation and detection strategies have been developed, and existing works explore the insertion of HTs in the space between standard cells in an integrated circuit. However, there is a lack of research into the vulnerabilities posed by the building blocks of most digital designs on the market today, the standard cells. This work investigates a novel threat model in which standard cells are considered untrusted. Our proposed threat model provides the design house with a tampered standard cell library. The intended netlist is synthesized and implemented using the tampered library. During fabrication, a nefarious foundry replaces the library's deactivated HT cells with activated counterparts. Using open-source and industry-standard Electronic Design Automation (EDA) tools, existing standard cell libraries, Saed32nm and Sky130nm, are converted into malicious libraries capable of masking the presence of arbitrary HTs from IC designers. The malicious library is then applied and characterized in multiple standard benchmark designs. To demonstrate the efficacy and stealthiness of this standard cell-based attack vector, three benchmark circuits, an AES-128 encryption core, an Ethernet controller, and a WISHBONE DMA engine, were synthesized using both clean and Trojan-infected libraries across Synopsys 32nm and SkyWater 130nm technologies. Design-level features, including total cell count, total area, dynamic power consumption, and static power, were extracted from these synthesized circuits to serve as inputs for binary classification

</details>


### [65] [VeriChat: An Agentic Conversational AI Assistant for Hardware Security Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01668)
*Dipayan Saha,Khan Thamid Hasan,Shams Tarek,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VeriChat是一个面向硬件安全验证的领域专用对话助手，采用检索增强的多智能体工作流，结合开源EDA工具，在问题回答（Faithfulness得分87.73%）和硬件木马检测等任务中显著优于通用大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件安全验证过程复杂，工程师缺乏结构化的安全指导支持；通用聊天机器人（如ChatGPT、Gemini）存在幻觉问题和知识静态过时的问题，无法可靠地用于硬件安全验证场景。

Method: 提出VeriChat系统：1) 采用检索增强生成（RAG）结合多智能体工作流，三个专用智能体协同工作以减少幻觉并提升响应的透明性和可靠性；2) 集成开源EDA工具（Icarus Verilog、Yosys、SymbiYosys），可直接对用户提供的RTL设计执行语法检查、综合分析、仿真和形式化验证。

Result: 在综合评估方法下，VeriChat的Faithfulness得分达到87.73%，显著优于主流闭源商业模型。在AES S-Box IP硬件木马检测案例中，系统能够通过多轮对话自主识别、仿真并形式化证明隐蔽的密钥泄露漏洞。

Conclusion: VeriChat作为一种补充而非替代现有验证工作流的领域专用对话助手，通过检索增强和多智能体协作有效缓解了幻觉问题，并通过EDA工具集成实现了从对话到实际硬件验证的闭环能力。

Abstract: Hardware security verification is a multi-stage process in which engineers must navigate complex design analyses, threat considerations, and verification strategies. They often need security-focused guidance, yet current verification environments provide little structured support for such assistance. Although conversational AI could offer such on-demand assistance, directly using general-purpose chatbots like ChatGPT or Gemini is risky due to their tendency to hallucinate and their reliance on static, outdated knowledge. We present VeriChat, a domain-specialized conversational assistant designed to support, rather than replace, existing verification workflows by providing context-aware security guidance. VeriChat employs a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent workflow in which three specialized agents collaboratively minimize hallucinations while improving the transparency and reliability of the response. Beyond question answering, VeriChat integrates open-source EDA tools, including Icarus Verilog, Yosys, and SymbiYosys, to perform syntax checking, synthesis analysis, simulation, and formal verification directly on user-provided RTL designs. Evaluated using a comprehensive methodology, VeriChat achieves a Faithfulness score of 87.73%, significantly outperforming the leading proprietary models. We demonstrate the framework through a hardware Trojan detection case study on an AES S-Box IP, where VeriChat autonomously identifies, simulates, and formally proves a covert key-leakage vulnerability through a multi-turn conversational workflow.

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Gradient-Based Attacks: Adversarial Robustness and Explainability Stability in Cybersecurity Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01679)
*Mona Rajhans,Vishal Khawarey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对网络安全分类器的对抗攻击问题，引入可解释性稳定性指数（ESI）来量化对抗扰动下TreeSHAP归因漂移，发现预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是两个需要联合评估的独立维度，并通过两轴框架（梯度依赖性与查询效率）解释了不同攻击方法在树集成模型上的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗攻击不仅会降低网络安全分类器的预测性能，还会破坏安全分析师依赖的SHAP解释稳定性，而现有研究主要关注预测鲁棒性，忽视了对抗扰动对模型可解释性的影响。

Method: 将之前MLP会议研究扩展到Random Forest和XGBoost，使用四个表格安全数据集（phishing URLs、UNSW-NB15、NF-ToN-IoT、HIKARI-2021），评估五种攻击方法（包括三种适用于不可微树模型的黑盒方法），提出ESI指标从TreeSHAP归因漂移角度量化可解释性稳定性，并构建梯度依赖性与查询效率两轴分析框架。

Result: 基于梯度的ZOO攻击对XGBoost产生虚假的预测鲁棒性（RI~0.98），而基于得分的Square Attack揭示了真实脆弱性（RI~0.36）；但伪扰动仍导致XGBoost的ESI仅为0.06-0.16，RF为0.14-0.29，说明预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是独立维度；发现PGD在z-score标准化表格数据上的反常现象通过步长消融实验得到解释。

Conclusion: 预测鲁棒性和解释稳定性是两个正交的评估维度，联合测量（RI+ESI）对网络安全分类器的对抗安全性评估至关重要；两轴攻击分类框架为树集成模型的对抗评估提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on cybersecurity classifiers pose a dual threat: degrading predictions and destabilising the SHAP-based explanations that security analysts rely on to understand and triage alerts. We extend our prior MLP conference study to Random Forest and XGBoost across four tabular security datasets (phishing URLs, UNSW-NB15, NF-ToN-IoT, HIKARI-2021), evaluating five attacks including three black-box methods applicable to non-differentiable tree models. We introduce the Explainability Stability Index (ESI), a scalar metric computed from TreeSHAP attribution drift under adversarial perturbation, reported on the same [0,1] scale as the Robustness Index (RI). A key finding is that gradient-based black-box attacks (ZOO) produce degenerate results against XGBoost (apparent RI ~0.98) due to piecewise-constant prediction surfaces, while score-based Square Attack reveals genuine vulnerability (RI ~0.36). These degenerate perturbations still drive substantial attribution drift: XGBoost ESI ~0.06-0.16 despite near-perfect ZOO robustness, versus 0.14-0.29 for RF, showing that prediction robustness and explanation stability are distinct axes requiring joint measurement. A two-axis framework (gradient dependence, query efficiency) explains the observed attack ranking and yields practical guidance for tree ensemble evaluation. A step-size ablation explains a counterintuitive PGD anomaly on z-score normalised tabular data.

</details>


### [67] [Pmeta-TLA: Backdoor Attacks for Speech Classification Models via Meta-Learning with Timbre Leakage Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01702)
*Yueming Huang,Wenhan Yao,Fen Xiao,Xiarun Chen,Weiping Wen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA)的语音后门攻击方法，通过帧级音色信息嵌入实现隐蔽攻击，并引入Pmeta-TLA机制实现单次训练嵌入多个后门，实验表明该方法在攻击效果、隐蔽性、鲁棒性和成本方面均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音分类模型面临后门攻击的安全威胁，现有语音触发器容易被深度神经网络防御器检测，因此亟需探索新的攻击技术以揭示和防范此类风险。

Method: 提出TLA攻击，将音色信息以帧级别散布到深度自监督特征中，生成自然感知的中毒样本；进一步提出Pmeta-TLA，利用元学习与Projected Conflicting Gradients (PCGrad)实现单次训练嵌入多个后门的多目标攻击。

Result: 在关键词识别任务上使用多种深度神经网络模型进行数据投毒后门攻击实验，结果表明所提方法在攻击效能、隐蔽性、鲁棒性和攻击成本方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: TLA及Pmeta-TLA是一种更隐蔽、更高效、更鲁棒且成本更低的语音后门攻击方案，对当前语音模型的安全防护提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Recently, speech classification methods have gained widespread adoption in intelligent gadgets. Current study indicates that backdoor attacks provide a substantial security concern to these models, underscoring the pressing necessity to investigate additional potential attack techniques to expose and prevent such risks. This work discusses the vulnerability of current speech triggers to detection by deep neural network defenders and introduces the Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA). The suggested trigger disseminates timbre information at the frame level within the deep self-supervised features, producing poisoned samples that appear natural to human perception. Furthermore, we introduce Pmeta-TLA, an innovative training mechanism for embedding numerous backdoors one time. This method proposes a multi-backdoor injection training strategy using meta-learning and Projected Conflicting Gradients (PCGrad) and introduces TLA as a multi-target attack tool within it. We performed tests on data-poisoning backdoor attacks in keyword spotting tasks utilizing some deep neural network models. Experimental results indicate that the proposed strategy attains superior Attack efficacy, enhanced stealthiness, robustness, and a reduced attack cost relative to baseline methods.

</details>


### [68] [Trust Boundary Semantic Gaps: A Multi-dimensional Analysis and Mitigation for Security-by-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01711)
*Doyeon Kim,Jin-Young Choi,Junghee Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了信任边界语义鸿沟（TBSG）概念，指跨信任边界的工件通过了正确的语法验证，但未能满足接收域的语义安全需求。通过分析75起安全事件（2014-2025），构建了四维分析模型MDTBSG（身份、空间、时间、解释），并开发了TBSAM设计时框架用于识别、优先排序和缓解TBSG。在SolarWinds/SUNBURST攻击上的回顾性应用表明，该方法能显式化接收域假设并识别中断攻击路径的控制措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的格式、协议和签名机制仅验证工件的语法合规性，但无法保证工件满足接收域的语义安全属性，导致通过了语法验证的恶意工件仍可造成安全事件。这种"信任边界语义鸿沟"（TBSG）缺乏系统化的识别和缓解方法。

Method: (1) 分析75起公开安全事件（2014-2025），归纳语义错配的四维模型MDTBSG：身份、空间、时间、解释；(2) 基于该模型开发TBSAM设计时框架，从设计规范中识别TBSG、优先排序、追溯传播至源头边界，并映射到架构控制措施；(3) 对SolarWinds/SUNBURST供应链攻击进行回顾性应用验证。

Result: TBSAM能够在设计阶段显式化接收域假设，区分本地起源和传播性语义鸿沟，识别出能够中断攻击路径的架构控制措施。在SolarWinds案例中成功展示了该方法如何将隐式假设显式化并阻断攻击链路。

Conclusion: 信任边界的语法验证是必要但不充分的，将信任边界假设显式化可以补充安全设计（Security-by-Design），为预防TBSG类漏洞提供系统化方法。

Abstract: Modern systems use format-, protocol-, and signature-based mechanisms before accepting artifacts across trust boundaries. These mechanisms are necessary: they show that an artifact is well formed, protocol-compliant, or properly authenticated. They do not, however, show that the artifact satisfies the semantic security properties required by the receiving domain. A signed update or an authenticated token may therefore be accepted yet enable compromise. We call this condition a Trust Boundary Semantic Gap (TBSG): an artifact crosses a trust boundary and passes correctly implemented syntactic validation, but the assertions established by that pass are insufficient to satisfy the receiving domain's security requirements. TBSG concerns what remains unestablished after a syntactic pass, not absent checks or implementation bugs. Analyzing 75 publicly reported security incidents (2014-2025) at the boundary level, we organize semantic misalignment into a four-dimensional analysis model: Identity, Spatial, Temporal, and Interpretation (MDTBSG). Building on it, we develop Trust Boundary Semantic Analysis and Mitigation (TBSAM), a design-time framework that identifies TBSGs from design specifications, prioritizes them, traces propagated gaps to their originating boundary, and maps each to candidate architectural controls. We apply TBSAM to a retrospective reconstruction of the SolarWinds/SUNBURST supply-chain attack, showing how it makes receiving-domain assumptions explicit, separates locally originating from propagated gaps, and identifies controls that interrupt the path. These results suggest that syntactic validation, while necessary, is not sufficient at trust boundaries, and that making trust-boundary assumptions explicit can complement Security-by-Design.

</details>


### [69] [Resilient Liquid Democracy: Mitigating Voting Power Imbalances via Secure Delegation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01730)
*Zhuolun Li,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全的液态民主机制，使用去中心化定时释放加密隐藏委托选择以防止从众和胁迫，同时引入排名多委托和个人回退选票以应对代表失败。在多个真实数据集上的评估表明，委托对代表性准确性的改善遵循"可恢复差距定律"，密封形成主要降低权力集中度而非直接提升准确性，排名多委托与个人回退选票在代表失败时显著降低选票损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有液态民主部署通常依赖透明委托，导致选民因流行度驱动而从众、胁迫可验证，以及高支持率代表弃权时引发系统性脆弱性。本文旨在解决知情专家路由与系统稳健性之间的张力。

Method: 采用去中心化定时释放加密实现密封委托，在形成阶段隐藏委托选择以防止从众和胁迫，最终投票阶段恢复公开可审计性。扩展协议引入排名多委托和个人回退选票以应对代表失败。通过形式化证明预揭示保密性和重新提交收据无关性，并在四个真实数据集上评估。

Result: 委托改善代表性准确性遵循"可恢复差距定律"：仅在弃权票大规模且系统性不具代表性时有帮助，否则中性或有害；代表制委托比委托给能力精英更安全。密封形成主要降低权力集中度而非直接提升准确性。排名多委托与个人回退选票在现实和针对性代表失败条件下显著降低选票损失，结果在二十个选举中可重复。

Conclusion: 密封委托机制可有效解决液态民主中从众、胁迫和代表失败问题，但委托的适用性存在边界条件，其价值在于结构性的权力分散而非直接的准确性提升。

Abstract: Liquid democracy promises to improve collective decision-making by allowing voters to vote directly, delegate their voting power to trusted participants, or combine both approaches through fallback mechanisms. However, existing deployments typically rely on transparent delegation, which exposes voters to popularity-driven herding, makes coercion verifiable, and introduces systemic fragility when highly-backed delegates abstain. In this paper, we propose a secure liquid democracy mechanism that resolves the tension between informed expertise routing and systemic robustness. We introduce a sealed delegation regime using decentralized timed-release encryption, which cryptographically hides delegation choices during the formation phase to prevent herding and coercion, while restoring full public auditability for the final tally. To address delegate failures, we extend the protocol with ranked multi-delegation and personal fallback ballots. We formally prove pre-reveal secrecy and resubmission receipt-freeness for our protocol. Finally, we evaluate the mechanism on four real datasets, a municipal participatory-budgeting election with a calibration survey, twenty further participatory-budgeting elections, and 60,000 US voters with an objective competence measure. They show that whether delegation improves representational accuracy follows a recoverable-gap law; it helps only when abstention is large and systematically unrepresentative, and is otherwise neutral or harmful, with representative-style delegation safer than delegating to a competence elite. The benefit of sealed formation is primarily structural, sharply reducing power concentration rather than directly improving accuracy; and ranked multi-delegation with personal fallback ballots sharply reduces vote loss under realistic and targeted delegate failures, a result that replicates across all twenty elections.

</details>


### [70] [Knowledge Over Parameters: Evolving Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01742)
*Yuqiang Sun,Han Liu,Ying Li,Yiran Zhang,Zong Cao,Ziyun Guo,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvoVuln 是一个自动化框架，将智能合约漏洞检测重构为程序性知识演化问题，仅需极少标注样本即可合成并优化检测逻辑。它通过 IoC 架构将检测规则编译为可执行策略，并采用两阶段演化流程（冷启动+少样本演化），在五种真实漏洞类型上取得 71% 的宏平均 F1 分数，且演化后的知识可跨模型迁移，单次演化成本低于 50 美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的智能合约漏洞检测方法存在两大瓶颈：基于提示的方法依赖人工设计的检测规则，而微调方法需要大规模标注数据集，但此类数据在该领域天然稀缺。因此，需要一种能自动合成和优化检测逻辑、且仅需极少标注样本的框架。

Method: EvoVuln 提出两个核心机制：(1) 运行时 IoC 架构——将检测规则编译为可执行策略，严格解耦确定性控制流与 LLM 语义推理，生成密集诊断遥测数据用于精确定位错误；(2) 两阶段演化流程——冷启动阶段利用自动合成的边界用例对初始规则进行引导和压力测试，少样本演化阶段仅用每个漏洞类型 5 个易受攻击和 5 个安全示例，通过溯因语义调试在不更新参数的情况下优化策略。

Result: 在五种真实漏洞类型上，EvoVuln 取得 71% 的宏平均 F1 分数，超越所有基线。演化后的程序性知识可跨模型迁移：使轻量低成本模型比大规模零样本模型高出 19 个百分点，且无需重新训练即可迁移至其他 LLM，单次演化成本低于 50 美元。

Conclusion: EvoVuln 证明了将漏洞检测转化为程序性知识演化问题的有效性，为资源受限场景下的智能合约安全分析提供了一种低成本、高迁移性的解决方案。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities are predominantly logic bugs whose detection requires structured, step-by-step procedural knowledge of attack patterns and contract semantics. Existing LLM-based methods struggle to generate this knowledge automatically: prompt-based methods rely on manually crafted detection rules, while fine-tuning requires massive labeled datasets that are inherently scarce in this domain. We present EvoVuln, an automated framework that reformulates vulnerability detection as a procedural knowledge evolution problem, synthesizing and refining detection logic using only a minimal number of labeled samples.
  To achieve this, EvoVuln introduces two key mechanisms. First, a Runtime with an Inversion of Control (IoC) architecture compiles detection rules into Executable Policies. This strictly decouples deterministic control flow from LLM semantic reasoning, ensuring faithful logical adherence and producing dense diagnostic telemetry for precise error localization. Second, a two-phase evolution pipeline refines the rule via abductive semantic debugging without any parameter updates: Cold Start bootstraps and stress-tests an initial rule using auto-synthesized corner cases; Few-Shot Evolving then grounds the policy in real-world semantics using only five vulnerable and five safe examples per vulnerability type.
  Evaluated across five real-world vulnerability types, EvoVuln achieves a 71% macro-average F1-score, outperforming all baselines. The evolved procedural knowledge is portable across models: it enables a lightweight, low-cost model to surpass a much larger zero-shot model by 19 percentage points, and transfers to other LLMs without retraining, at a one-time evolution cost under $50.

</details>


### [71] [Has This Checkpoint Been Abliterated? A Two-Signal Audit and Its Failure Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01854)
*Gabriel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需阈值的检查点审计方法，结合参考锚定激活拒绝差距和权重恢复能量两个互补信号，在273个检查点注册表上以AUROC 0.95识别57个公开ablitation，显著优于单一信号，并分析了两种绕过攻击（欺骗参考和白盒训练绕过）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台在部署前无法判断开放权重检查点的拒绝机制是否被移除；运行时防护仅评分生成内容而非构件本身，因此需要一种预部署的审计方法。

Method: 结合两种廉价内部信号：参考锚定激活拒绝差距（提供拒绝特异性）和基础到候选权重差异的权重恢复能量（提供召回率），两者负相关且标签互补，通过z-sum进行无阈值检查点审计。

Result: 在273个检查点注册表（涵盖Qwen、DeepSeek-distilled Qwen、Llama和Gemma）上，z-sum以AUROC 0.95分离57个公开ablitation和37个良性微调/合并/指令微调，显著优于单一信号的0.84和0.90；Youden校准阈值在保留族上达到平衡准确率0.89（FPR 0.11），仅漏检4/57。

Conclusion: 该审计方法是有效的分类手段而非防篡改方案，依赖于经认证的参考，其主张受限于评估所用的注册表。映射了两种失败模式：欺骗参考（无需训练）和白盒所有者训练绕过阈值，后者严重性更高。

Abstract: Can a platform tell, before deployment, whether an open-weight checkpoint has had its refusal mechanism stripped? Runtime guards cannot: they score generations, not the artifact. We combine two cheap internal signals, a reference-anchored activation refusal-gap and a weight-recovery energy of the base-to-candidate weight difference, into a threshold-free checkpoint audit. The two are negatively correlated and label-complementary: the gap supplies refusal-specificity and the weight energy supplies recall. On a 273-checkpoint registry spanning Qwen, DeepSeek-distilled Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, their z-sum separates 57 public abliterations from 37 benign fine-tunes, merges, and instruction-tunes at AUROC 0.95, significantly above either signal alone (0.84, 0.90), and a Youden-calibrated threshold transfers to held-out families at balanced accuracy 0.89 (FPR 0.11), missing only 4 of 57. We then map two failures, in order of severity: a spoofed reference evades both axes with no training (ΔW=0, \r{ho}=1 by construction), and a white-box owner trains a checkpoint past the threshold while it stays guard-unsafe and coherent. The audit is effective triage, not tamper-proofing: it presumes an attested reference, and its claims are bounded by the registry we evaluate it on.

</details>


### [72] [Behind the Refusal: Determining Guardrail Activation via Behavioral Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02121)
*William Hackett,Peter Garraghan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出首个黑盒防护栏侦察方法，通过HTTP、词汇和时序信号行为监测，以100%准确率检测AI系统中是否存在防护栏，并区分防护栏拦截与LLM拒绝，平均F1达98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗性模拟中，研究人员难以区分防护栏拦截与LLM安全对齐拒绝，两类绕过技术差异显著，影响攻击策略选择与优化。

Method: 在黑盒假设下（无先验知识），通过监测HTTP、词汇和时序信号的行为模式，探测目标AI系统中防护栏的存在性，并对恶意/良性交互进行统计学差异分析。

Result: 防护栏检测准确率100%，良性/恶意交互行为分离具有统计显著性（q<0.001）；区分防护栏拦截与LLM拒绝的平均F1分数为98%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效实现了对AI系统防护栏的黑盒侦察，为攻击方提供了关键信息，有助于针对性优化攻击策略。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) and agentic systems become integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their safety and security is critical. Guardrail systems that detect and block malicious instructions sent to and from an LLM are an essential component of AI security. However, researchers conducting black-box adversarial emulation against production AI systems often struggle to determine whether a guardrail block or an LLM rejection has occurred. This distinction is important because the techniques used to bypass guardrails can differ substantially from those used to bypass LLM safety alignment, and has a material impact on attack technique selection and optimization. We propose the first black-box guardrail reconnaissance methodology, which detects the presence of a guardrail within a target AI system through behavioral monitoring of HTTP, lexical, and timing signals, assuming only black-box access and zero prior knowledge of the guardrail or AI system. Experiments demonstrate that our approach detects guardrail presence with 100% accuracy, with statistically significant behavioral separation between benign and malicious interactions (q < 0.001). Our approach further identifies the content categories a guardrail is designed to block, and distinguishes guardrail blocks from LLM rejection on unseen prompts with an average F1 score of 98%.

</details>


### [73] [Cloak and Detonate: Scanner Evasion and Dynamic Detection of Agent Skill Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02357)
*Zimo Ji,Congying Xu,Zongjie Li,Yudong Gao,Xin Wei,Shuai Wang,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM编码智能体的第三方技能市场供应链攻击面，提出了一种规避现有静态扫描器的伪装框架SkillCloak（成功率超90%），并在此基础上设计了基于运行时行为审计的防御系统SkillDetonate（检测率97%，误报率2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体依赖第三方市场技能，这些技能拥有智能体的全部权限，形成软件供应链攻击面；而现有基于静态扫描的防御（模式匹配或LLM-as-judge）无法抵御保持恶意语义不变、仅改变载荷外观的自适应规避攻击。

Method: 提出SkillCloak规避框架，包含两种互补策略：结构混淆（将可见载荷指标重写为语义等价形式）和自提取技能打包（将恶意组件从安装时视图隐藏，在智能体执行期间恢复）。在此基础上提出SkillDetonate，一种基于行为中心的运行时审计器，在沙箱中执行技能，通过OS边界信息流证据检测恶意效果，结合按需闭包提升（观察执行期间实例化的指令）和基于标记的污点分析（追踪智能体上下文、文件、进程和网络操作中的敏感数据流）。

Result: 在8个扫描器和1,613个野外恶意技能上，SFS打包绕过所有扫描器成功率超90%，结构混淆绕过大部分静态扫描器超80%，混合扫描器达96%。SkillDetonate检测97%的攻击（2%误报率），在真实恶意技能上维持87%检测率。

Conclusion: 基于外观的审计不足以防御自适应规避攻击，需要转向基于运行时行为检测的防御范式。

Abstract: LLM coding agents increasingly rely on third-party agent skills from public marketplaces, which execute with the agent's privileges and create a software supply-chain attack surface: a malicious skill can steal credentials, exfiltrate source code, or install backdoors. Existing defenses use static skill scanners based on pattern matching or LLM-as-judge analysis, but it remains unclear whether they withstand adaptive evasions that preserve malicious behavior while changing payload appearance.
  This paper first presents an adversarial study of existing skill scanners through SkillCloak, a payload-preserving evasion framework that keeps the attack semantics intact while transforming their visible form. SkillCloak uses two complementary strategies: Structural Obfuscation, which rewrites visible payload indicators into semantically equivalent forms, and Self-Extracting Skill (SFS) Packing, which hides malicious components from the install-time view and restores them during agent execution. Across eight scanners and 1,613 in-the-wild malicious skills, SFS Packing bypasses every scanner at over 90%, while Structural Obfuscation bypasses over 80% on most static scanners and reaches 96% on a hybrid scanner, showing that appearance-based auditing is insufficient.
  Motivated by this finding, we propose SkillDetonate, a behavior-centric runtime auditor that executes skills in a sandbox and detects malicious effects through OS-boundary information-flow evidence rather than install-time appearance. SkillDetonate combines on-demand closure lift, which observes instructions materialized during execution, with marker-based taint analysis, which tracks sensitive-data flows across the agent context, files, processes, and network operations. The results show that SkillDetonate detects 97% of attacks at a 2% false-positive rate and sustains 87% detection on real-world malicious skills.

</details>


### [74] [SoK: A Taxonomy for Cybersecurity Incident Response Influence Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02451)
*Thomas Biege,Marius Brockhoff,Jonas Kaspereit,Fabian Ising,Lea Gröber,Sebastian Schinzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述417篇学术文献和40篇非科学出版物，构建了“网络安全事件响应影响因素分类体系”（CIR-IF Taxonomy），系统化整理了1999年至2024年中影响组织网络安全事件准备和响应的因素，并与现有框架比较证明了其更丰富、更严谨、更系统的组织视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件响应是研究与实践的关键领域，但现有文献缺乏统一框架来系统组织积累的知识，且事件响应涉及技术、人机交互、组织理论和人为因素等多个领域，需要一个综合性的整合视角来识别未充分探索的领域并更有效地指导实证和理论研究。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，对417篇学术文献和40篇非科学出版物进行梳理，推导出CIR-IF Taxonomy；将已有实证发现归类至该分类体系中；并将该分类体系与七个现有科学框架及NIST SP 800-61r3事件响应配置文件中的NIST网络安全框架元素进行系统比较。

Result: CIR-IF Taxonomy在影响因素的组织视角上比现有七大学术框架和NIST框架更为丰富、严谨和系统化，为1999年至2024年间的知识提供了全面、最新的概览。

Conclusion: 该分类体系为网络安全事件响应领域提供了一个统一的、整合性的知识组织工具，有助于研究者识别研究空白并更有效地开展实证与理论研究。

Abstract: Cybersecurity incident response has emerged as a critical area of interest for both researchers and practitioners. The corpus of literature on cybersecurity incident response is expanding, yet a unified framework for systematically organizing the accumulated knowledge remains absent. The aspects of incident response span multiple domains, including technology, human-computer interaction, organizational theory, and human factors. A comprehensive, integrative perspective on these factors can enable researchers to identify underexplored areas and more effectively target their empirical and theoretical investigations. Our study systematizes the factors that influence organizational preparedness for and response to cybersecurity incidents. Through a systematic review of academic literature (n = 417) and non-scientific publications (n = 40), we derived the "Cybersecurity Incident Response Influencing Factor Taxonomy" (\textit{CIR-IF Taxonomy}). Existing empirical findings were classified within this taxonomy, providing a comprehensive and up-to-date overview of knowledge from the period 1999 to mid-2024. The taxonomy categories were systematically compared with seven established scientific frameworks and with the \textit{NIST Cyber Security Framework} elements referenced in the \textit{NIST Special Publication 800-61r3} incident response profile. The results of this comparison show that the \textit{CIR-IF Taxonomy} delivers a richer, more rigorous, and more systematically organized view of the factors that drive and shape incident response.

</details>

### [75] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PACE，一种模块化神经符号框架，通过将神经网络预测与ASP符号推理结合，在反事实解释生成中显式编码领域知识和干预约束，产生符合可行性要求的可解释解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽能生成改变预测结果的替代方案，但缺乏显式机制融入领域知识和干预约束，往往产生不现实或不可行的建议。

Method: 提出PACE框架，将预测与推理分离为两个组件：神经预测模型（分类器）和符号推理层（ASP规则）。符号推理层在反事实生成过程中强制执行领域特定约束，显式建模可行干预，且模型无关。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明，符号约束能生成更好满足领域可行性要求的解释，揭示了反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法为透明、可行性感知的反事实解释提供了一条有前景的路径，展现了在可解释AI中结合数据驱动与符号推理的潜力。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision.
